<section class="mt-12 mb-5" id="faq-section">
<div class="row justify-content-center">
<div class="col-md-10">
<div class="text-center mb-6 pb-4">
<h2 class="h3 font-weight-bold m-0">ETH.STORE® Explained</h2>
</div>

<div class="accordion" id="faqAccordion">
<div class="card ethstore-surface">
<div class="card-header" id="faq-header-1">
<h3 class="mb-0 w-100 h5">
<button class="accordion-trigger collapsed" type="button" data-toggle="collapse" data-target="#faq-collapse-1" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-collapse-1">
<span>What is ETH.STORE®?</span>
</button>
</h3>
</div>
<div id="faq-collapse-1" class="collapse" aria-labelledby="faq-header-1">
<div class="card-body pt-0">ETH.STORE® (Ether <strong>St</strong>aking <strong>O</strong>ffered <strong>R</strong>at<strong>e</strong>) is a transparent Ethereum staking reward reference rate. It represents the average financial return validators on the Ethereum network have achieved in a 24-hour period as published on <a href="/ethstore">beaconcha.in</a>.</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="card ethstore-surface">
<div class="card-header" id="faq-header-beaconscore">
<h3 class="mb-0 w-100 h5">
<button class="accordion-trigger collapsed" type="button" data-toggle="collapse" data-target="#faq-collapse-beaconscore" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-collapse-beaconscore">
<span>How does <span class="brand-mark">BeaconScore<sup>®</sup></span> compare to ETH.STORE®?</span>
</button>
</h3>
</div>
<div id="faq-collapse-beaconscore" class="collapse" aria-labelledby="faq-header-beaconscore">
<div class="card-body pt-0">ETH.STORE® measures yield — how much validators earned in absolute ETH returns over time. <span class="brand-mark">BeaconScore<sup>®</sup></span> measures efficiency — how much of the maximum possible reward they actually captured. Use ETH.STORE® to answer "what's the yield?"; use <span class="brand-mark">BeaconScore<sup>®</sup></span> to answer "how well did this operator perform?" <a href="/beaconscore">Learn more about <span class="brand-mark">BeaconScore<sup>®</sup></span></a>.</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="card ethstore-surface">
<div class="card-header" id="faq-header-2">
<h3 class="mb-0 w-100 h5">
<button class="accordion-trigger collapsed" type="button" data-toggle="collapse" data-target="#faq-collapse-2" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-collapse-2">
<span>How are active validators counted by ETH.STORE®?</span>
</button>
</h3>
</div>
<div id="faq-collapse-2" class="collapse" aria-labelledby="faq-header-2">
<div class="card-body pt-0">All validators that stayed in the active state during the entire reward day are counted. New validators joining or exiting mid day are excluded, as are validators whose effective balance changed during the day &mdash; the rate is measured against the effective balance held at the start of the day, so a mid-day change would leave that denominator ambiguous.</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="card ethstore-surface">
<div class="card-header" id="faq-header-3">
<h3 class="mb-0 w-100 h5">
<button class="accordion-trigger collapsed" type="button" data-toggle="collapse" data-target="#faq-collapse-3" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-collapse-3">
<span>How long is a reward day and when does it start?</span>
</button>
</h3>
</div>
<div id="faq-collapse-3" class="collapse" aria-labelledby="faq-header-3">
<div class="card-body pt-0">Each reward day starts at 12:00:23 UTC and lasts for 24 hours (or exactly 225 beaconchain epochs).</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="card ethstore-surface">
<div class="card-header" id="faq-header-4">
<h3 class="mb-0 w-100 h5">
<button class="accordion-trigger collapsed" type="button" data-toggle="collapse" data-target="#faq-collapse-4" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-collapse-4">
<span>How are total rewards calculated in ETH.STORE®?</span>
</button>
</h3>
</div>
<div id="faq-collapse-4" class="collapse" aria-labelledby="faq-header-4">
<div class="card-body pt-0">
<p>Total staking rewards consists of consensus rewards and transaction fees:</p>
<ol>
<li>Consensus rewards: Rewards received by validators for proposing and attesting in accordance with the consensus rules of the Ethereum protocol.</li>
<li>Transaction fees: Fees received by validators for processing transactions on the Ethereum network.</li>
</ol>
<p>Total staking rewards equals the sum of consensus rewards and transaction fees.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="card ethstore-surface">
<div class="card-header" id="faq-header-5">
<h3 class="mb-0 w-100 h5">
<button class="accordion-trigger collapsed" type="button" data-toggle="collapse" data-target="#faq-collapse-5" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-collapse-5">
<span>How can I reproduce the ETH.STORE® calculation on my own?</span>
</button>
</h3>
</div>
<div id="faq-collapse-5" class="collapse" aria-labelledby="faq-header-5">
<div class="card-body pt-0">
<div class="table-responsive">
<table class="table mb-0">
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Variable</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Start epoch (SE):</td>
<td>First epoch after 12:00 UTC every day</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>End epoch (EE):</td>
<td>SE + 225</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Active validators (VS):</td>
<td>Validators that have been active from start SE to end EE [SE,EE) and whose effective balance is unchanged across that range</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Sum rewards (RS):</td>
<td>Sum of all rewards (consensus, tx) in VS that have been generated in epochs [SE,EE)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Sum effective balances (EB):</td>
<td>Sum of all effective balances at epoch SE of all validators in VS</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>ETH.STORE®:</td>
<td>(RS/EB)*365</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>

<br />
<p>
Calculations for <strong><time class="ethstore-date" data-ethstore-format="dd MMMM yyyy" data-ethstore-timestamp="1790251223"></time> 12:00:23 UTC - <time class="ethstore-date" data-ethstore-format="dd MMMM yyyy" data-ethstore-timestamp="1790337623"></time> 12:00:22 UTC:</strong>
</p>

<div class="table-responsive">
<table class="table mb-0">
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Variable</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Start epoch (SE):</td>
<td>Epoch 477675</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>End epoch (EE):</td>
<td>Epoch 477900</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Active validators (VS):</td>
<td>in timeframe [477675,477900)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Sum rewards (RS):</td>
<td>3,046.447526315918
Ether</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Sum effective balances (EB):</td>
<td>4.1833065×10⁰⁷
Ether</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>ETH.STORE®:</td>
<td>2.65807285960355 % = (3,046.447526315918
/ (4.1833065×10⁰⁷
)) * 365</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="card ethstore-surface">
<div class="card-header" id="faq-header-6">
<h3 class="mb-0 w-100 h5">
<button class="accordion-trigger collapsed" type="button" data-toggle="collapse" data-target="#faq-collapse-6" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-collapse-6">
<span>Is the ETH.STORE® code Open-Source?</span>
</button>
</h3>
</div>
<div id="faq-collapse-6" class="collapse" aria-labelledby="faq-header-6">
<div class="card-body pt-0">Yes, you can run the calculations yourself with our open source ETH.STORE® tool and verify the integrity of the ETH.STORE® listed here. Check out <a href="https://github.com/gobitfly/eth.store/" target="_blank">ETH.STORE® on Github</a>.</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</section>

Currently Bitfly does not grant any right to access or use ETH.STORE® for such purpose.</span>

<script>
;(function () {
function wireCurrencyControl(element) {
if (!element) return
if (typeof currencyOptions !== "undefined" && currencyOptions.length) {
customElements.whenDefined(element.tagName.toLowerCase()).then(function () {
element.currencyOptions = currencyOptions
})
}
element.addEventListener("update:selected-currency", function (e) {
var val = Array.isArray(e.detail) ? e.detail[0] : e.detail
document.cookie = "currency=" + val + ";path=/;max-age=34560000;SameSite=Lax"
window.location.reload()
})
}

var sidebar = document.querySelector("bc-sidebar")
var topbar = document.querySelector("bc-sidebar-topbar")
var landingHeader = document.querySelector("bc-landing-header")
var shell = document.querySelector(".bc-v1-shell")
wireCurrencyControl(sidebar)
wireCurrencyControl(topbar)
wireCurrencyControl(landingHeader)

var liveChrome = [topbar, landingHeader].filter(Boolean)
if (liveChrome.length) {
var applyLatestState = function (data) {
var rates = data.rates || {}
var options = rates.mainCurrencyTickerPrices
? Object.entries(rates.mainCurrencyTickerPrices).map(function (entry) {
return {
currency: entry[1].label,
code: entry[0],
symbol: entry[1].symbol,
rate: entry[1].rate,
}
})
: null
liveChrome.forEach(function (element) {
if (typeof data.currentSlot === "number") element.currentSlot = data.currentSlot
if (typeof data.finalityDelay === "number") element.finalityDelay = data.finalityDelay
if (typeof data.syncing === "boolean") element.isSyncing = data.syncing
element.gasPriceGwei = data.gasPriceGwei
if (options && options.length) element.currencyOptions = options
})
}
var pollLatestState = function () {
fetch("/latestState")
.then(function (res) {
return res.ok ? res.json() : null
})
.then(function (data) {
if (data) applyLatestState(data)
})
.catch(function () {
})
}
setInterval(pollLatestState, 12000)
}

if (!sidebar || !topbar || !shell) return
topbar.addEventListener("toggleCollapsed", function (e) {
var collapsed = Array.isArray(e.detail) ? !!e.detail[0] : !!e.detail
var state = collapsed ? "rail" : "auto"
document.cookie = "bc-sidebar-collapsed=" + collapsed + ";path=/;max-age=34560000;SameSite=Lax"
sidebar.setAttribute("collapse-state", state)
topbar.setAttribute("collapse-state", state)
shell.classList.remove("bc-v1-shell--auto", "bc-v1-shell--rail", "bc-v1-shell--expanded")
shell.classList.add("bc-v1-shell--" + state)
})
})()
</script>

<script>
var currency = "ETH"

function slotToTime(slot) {
var gts = 1606824023
var sps = 12
return (gts + slot * sps) * 1000
}

function epochToTime(epoch) {
var gts = 1606824023
var sps = 12
var spe = 32
return (gts + epoch * sps * spe) * 1000
}

function timeToEpoch(ts) {
var gts = 1606824023
var sps = 12
var spe = 32
var slot = Math.floor((ts / 1000 - gts) / sps)
var epoch = Math.floor(slot / spe)
if (epoch < 0) return 0
return epoch
}

function timeToSlot(ts) {
var gts = 1606824023
var sps = 12
var spe = 32
var slot = Math.floor((ts / 1000 - gts) / sps)
if (slot < 0) return 0
return slot
}
</script>

<script>
function hideCookieBanner() {
$("#cookie-banner").attr("class", "d-none")
}

function acceptCookieBanner() {
document.cookie = "bc-cookies-preference=all;max-age=" + 60 * 60 * 24 * 365 + ";path=/"
hideCookieBanner()
}

function acceptOnlyNecessaryCookies() {
document.cookie = "bc-cookies-preference=functional;max-age=" + 60 * 60 * 24 * 365 + ";path=/"
hideCookieBanner()
}

} else {
$("#cookie-banner").attr("class", "")

<script>
function formatEthstoreDates() {
if (!window.luxon) {
return
}

document.querySelectorAll('[data-ethstore-timestamp]').forEach(function (elem) {
var timestamp = Number(elem.getAttribute('data-ethstore-timestamp'))
var format = elem.getAttribute('data-ethstore-format') || 'dd MMMM yyyy'

if (!Number.isFinite(timestamp)) {
return
}

var date = window.luxon.DateTime.fromMillis(timestamp * 1000).toUTC()

elem.textContent = date.toFormat(format)
elem.setAttribute('datetime', date.toISO())
})
}

formatEthstoreDates()

var hash = window.location.hash
if (hash && window.jQuery) {
var collapseEl = document.querySelector(hash)
if (collapseEl && collapseEl.classList.contains('collapse')) {
window.jQuery(collapseEl).collapse('show')
}
}

function gtag() {
dataLayer.push(arguments)
}

function createFunctionWithTimeout(callback, opt_timeout) {
var called = false
function fn() {
if (!called) {
called = true
callback()
}
}
setTimeout(fn, opt_timeout || 1000)
return fn
}

function handleOutboundLinkClicks(event) {
var link = event.currentTarget.href
event.preventDefault()
var fallback = setTimeout(function () {
window.location = link
}, 1000)

gtag('event', 'click', {
event_category: 'Outbound Link',
event_label: link,
event_callback: function () {
clearTimeout(fallback)
window.location = link
}
})
}

var outbounds = document.querySelectorAll('[ga-outbound]')
for (var i = 0; i < outbounds.length; i++) {
var element = outbounds[i]
element.addEventListener('click', handleOutboundLinkClicks)
}

<script>
const configurations = null

function makeExecutableFragment(htmlText) {
const template = document.createElement("template")
template.innerHTML = htmlText

const scripts = Array.from(template.content.querySelectorAll("script"))
scripts.forEach((oldScript) => {
const script = document.createElement("script")
Array.from(oldScript.attributes).forEach((attr) => script.setAttribute(attr.name, attr.value))
script.textContent = oldScript.textContent
oldScript.replaceWith(script)
})

return template.content
}

function markFragmentWithConfigId(fragment, configId) {
if (!configId) {
return
}

Array.from(fragment.childNodes).forEach((node) => {
if (node.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE) {
node.setAttribute("data-ad-config-id", configId)
}
})
}

function initiateConfig(config, retry = false) {
const container = $(config.JQuerySelector)[0]
if (container) {
const htmlText = config.HtmlContent?.trim()
if (!htmlText) {
console.warn(`Ad configuration ${config.Id} has empty HtmlContent`)
return
}

const fragment = makeExecutableFragment(htmlText)
if (!fragment.firstElementChild) {
console.warn(`Ad configuration ${config.Id} has no root element`)
return
}
markFragmentWithConfigId(fragment, config.Id)

switch (config.InsertMode) {
case "replace":
container.replaceWith(fragment)
break
case "before":
container.before(fragment)
break
case "after":
container.after(fragment)
break
default:
container.append(fragment)
break
}
} else if (retry) {
setTimeout(() => initiateConfig(config), 1000)
} else {
console.log(`Ad component ${config.JQuerySelector} not found`)
}
}

window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
configurations?.forEach((config) => initiateConfig(config, true))
})
</script>
